### 2026-06-17 11:50 — Engine interface + baseline build
- **Summary:** Added gridq.engine: the shared OptimisationEngine interface (Protocol) and BaselineEngine — a deliberately limited, integration-only engine. It returns a schema-valid OptimisationResult that is always implementation_level=baseline / bankable_status=not_bankable, invents NO revenues (all value streams 0 with warnings), sizes illustratively (10% of max_import_kw), and reports solver_status=insufficient_data when max_import_kw is missing. Added SolverStatus.INSUFFICIENT_DATA (additive). engine/README.md explains where the MILP plugs in. No spoof real engine, no pricing maths.
- **Files changed:** src/gridq/engine/{interface,baseline,errors,__init__}.py + README.md, src/gridq/schemas/enums.py (added SolverStatus.INSUFFICIENT_DATA), tests/test_engine_interface.py, tests/test_baseline_engine.py, tests/conftest.py

- **Remaining tasks:** Milestone 3 cont.: scenario engine (M6) + finance wrapper (M5) on the contracts; later the real MILP implementing OptimisationEngine.
- **Warnings for next person:** BaselineEngine is plumbing ONLY — never show its numbers to capital. BASELINE_POWER_FRACTION (0.1) is an integration heuristic, not a library assumption (see OPEN_COMMENTS).

### 2026-11-23 22:49 — Olly
- **Summary:** Roadmap WP-THERM-04 (spec A4-04, B6-04, TF-05): wrote the cooling-flex baseline method v1 for pre-registration, docs/methods/cooling_flex_baseline_method_v1.md, sha256:a1142decabea81ee7a7803c3829ae6ebe8a555db9fb56ffeab60c9d075dd421e. Documents only. It measures an IC-11 1.0.0 surface: sub-metered cooling plant (IPMVP Option B); two-stage regression (cooling load on IT load and dry-bulb, plant power on load and ambient) fitted and hashed before each UTC month; additive same-day adjustment over a 3 h window ending 1 h before start, capped at 10 %; gates hourly CV(RMSE) <= 30 % and NMBE <= 10 % in and out of sample and per IC-11 cell, plus a coverage gate; uncertainty from placebo windows, event verified only at FSU68 <= 50 %; delivered kW is the lowest 15-minute block against the accepted kW because a declared kW is firm; rebound measured and netted; a thermal breach or an unverified event is credited 0; re-baselining is the only non-routine remedy; BL01 compared row by row with dual reporting. Lists 15 metered and 15 BMS quantities for WP-LEDGER-04 to bind.
- **Files changed:** docs/methods/cooling_flex_baseline_method_v1.md, docs/changelog.md, docs/shared_work/HANDOFF_LOG.md
- **Tests run:** none (documents only). shasum -a 256 of the method file recorded in docs/changelog.md
- **Remaining tasks:** Coordinator: review, then tag this commit before any trial data exists; the method file must not change after the tag (any change is v2). WP-LEDGER-04: register the hash as a self-issued record (ADR-D10), bind Q-01..Q-15 and B-01..B-15 to NEW-IC-14 fields, implement the BL01 replicas. WP-THERM-09: monthly report layout (Cyprus format not seen). Owner: supply ASHRAE Guideline 14-2014 and IPMVP Core Concepts 2022 so the secondary-sourced thresholds can be checked before the tag; confirm what the spec means by Ofgem's 2027 baselining review; decide how many supporting events raise a cell's evidence class; archive retention period; whether an independent party should approve the method.
- **Warnings for next person:** ASHRAE Guideline 14 and IPMVP were NOT read: the 30 %/10 % gates come from FEMP M&V Guidelines 4.0 Table 4-2 (stated there for calibrated simulation), the 50 % at 68 % limit and the 90-110 % range rule from arXiv:1801.08175, the FSU formula from a BPA/SBW 2017 paraphrase. No formal Ofgem 2027 baselining review was found, only the open letter of 3 Nov 2026. No wet-bulb formula is registered (Q-12 is taken as the instrument reports it). docs/bess_constants_register.md says the BL01 in-day window runs up to 3 hours before gate closure; Elexon's Baselining Methodology Document v3.0 section 3.4.3 says it is the three hours ending AT Gate Closure, and non-working days use the middle two of four days, not a four-day mean. Not corrected here (out of this package's scope).

### 2026-11-26 09:05 — Olly
- **Summary:** Roadmap WP-SITE-01 on roadmap/wp-site01-dcload: new plug-in package gridq.datacentre, the data-centre load and PUE synthesiser. It produces a pre-operational load series at half-hourly and one-minute resolution, with pointwise P05/P10/P50/P90/P95 bands, each band edge carrying a 95% order-statistic interval. The model sums IT ramp x utilisation, UPS loss (in loss space, N/N+1/2N), distribution loss, auxiliaries and cooling (WP-THERM-04 s6 C1-C3 forms, free-cooling regimes at T*, coefficients or duty points), driven by an ambient input series; PUE is an output. Sampling is a seeded two-layer Latin hypercube (N required, at least 72). The expected series is the mean; aggregates are computed per draw; attribution is a ranked data ask. The one-minute series is an AR(1) conditioned exactly on half-hour means, with the exact model re-centred per block. The series hash covers the outputs plus a manifest. data_centre is registered by load_plugin('gridq.datacentre.plugin') as a DataCentreArchetype(ReferenceProfile) with no day shape. The WS-SITE route derive_data_centre_half_hourly stamps CORE's modelled-HH fields and a MEDIUM-ceiling DerivedAssumption. No CORE path, pyproject line or golden was touched. Decisions D81-D84 (Fable 5.2).
- **Files changed:** src/gridq/datacentre/{__init__,sources,distributions,inputs,presets,model,synthesiser,archetype,plugin,derive}.py,tests/_datacentre_support.py,tests/test_datacentre_{registration,series,reproducibility,model,data_rule}.py,docs/decisions.md,docs/changelog.md,docs/shared_work/HANDOFF_LOG.md
- **Tests run:** fast tier (run_tests.sh fast): 4372 passed, 12 skipped, 0 failed (388 s); 48 new datacentre tests green; mutations M1-M5 each red then restored green; ruff check clean, ruff format --check clean (450 files), mypy clean (189 files); python -m gridq.contracts --check ok
- **Remaining tasks:** Bands stay uncalibrated until the spec 16.2 post-energisation back-test. WP-SITE-04 should consume member draws via Synthesis.one_minute, not band edges. The ambient series waits on the Atlas ERA5 work package. Data requests are listed in gridq.datacentre.presets.DATA_REQUESTS (utilisation, ramp, ups, distribution_loss, auxiliaries, cooling, variability, ambient) plus the fill and slip distributions. The Atlas roadmap and Build Map status for WP-SITE-01 were not updated from this worktree. Coordinator: merge roadmap/wp-site01-dcload.
- **Warnings for next person:** Every input is required with no numeric default (tests/test_datacentre_data_rule.py enforces this); do not add defaults. The only presets are S1 (LBNL-2001637) Table 4.3 and Table 3.1, opt-in. No ASHRAE figure is used anywhere. Never write modelled energy into load_data.annual_kwh, or CORE's scale-to-total route reopens. Importing gridq.datacentre registers nothing; call ensure_registered() or load_plugin. ### 2026-11-26 14:42 — Olly
- **Summary:** WP-UW-17 on roadmap/wp-uw17-finance (cut from p1-foundations afd7871): finance completeness. Rulings by Fable 5.2 are D128 to D132. (1) The price is levelised over the modelled delivered volume (fade and availability each year) on the run's own P50 pre-debt series at the target rate. hits_target_return is a closed loop: the price is added back and the equity or project IRR is measured; there is an equity-solved diagnostic. (2) financing.cpi_pct_per_year is read. When numeric, opex, insurance, metering, DNO and business rates escalate. Every line carries a basis tag, and a real ledger and real IRR are added. T-4 CM is paid U x E_t per SI 2014/2043 Sch 1 para 3(5) and 3(7). T-1 is held flat (para 3(6) omitted by SI 2025/917). U=1.0 with a data ask when no ONS CPI is given. When the key is absent the result is bit-identical; when null the run warns. (3) LifecycleInputs gives a dated monthly construction timeline. IDC is rolled into the principal at COD, and the IDC rate falls back to the term rate with a label. The COD delay is supplied via generate_quote(lifecycle=), gridq run --lifecycle and the app's lifecycle field, as an applied zero-weight cod_delay row. (4) Terminal value, decommissioning and replacements are modelled or excluded by name with a data request. TV is in the IRR, never CFADS. A ledger is always populated and reaches the pack. IC-12 chassis 2.2.0 is an additive minor; v2.1.0 is archived by sha256. Demo golden numbers are unmoved (gb_v1_4 has no CPI key). The rich_quote fixture's N12 zero price becomes 0.021967 (IRR 7.41% < 12%), and test_consulting_report was updated.
